# 1000 AI Affiliate Programs

A browsable research directory of **1,001 distinct AI-related affiliate, partner, and referral program URLs**. It covers writing and research, generative media, marketing, coding, automation, productivity, education, and specialist software. Every entry includes a short product description, the main website when identifiable, a program or application link, commission details when public, and a research source.

**Research date:** October 19, 2026 · **Entries:** 1,001 · **Unique affiliate links:** 1,001

Affiliate terms change. Some programs require approval, publish rates only inside a partner dashboard, have conflicting public terms, or have closed. Review the linked source and the current program agreement before promoting a product. An `Unconfirmed` main site means the merchant identity could not be matched confidently; the affiliate program link is still provided for investigation.

<a id="how-to-use-this-directory"></a>
## How to use this directory

- **Company / product** and **What it does** identify the tool and its main use. Products are alphabetical within each broad category.
- **Main site** points to the company website when it could be identified confidently. **Affiliate program** points to the application or official program page; it is not a personal referral link.
- **Commission** records the public rate or explicitly says when it is unavailable. **Terms and evidence** gives the payment model, duration, cookie window, exceptions, and a source used during research.
- A directory listing, brand landing page, and partner portal can publish different terms. Where they disagree, the row flags the conflict and recommends checking the current agreement after signing in.

**Data quality:** 17 entries have no confidently identified main-site URL. The directory retains those entries with an explicit `Unconfirmed` label so a reader can distinguish missing evidence from a verified company link.

<a id="research-notes-and-corrections"></a>
## Research notes and corrections

This list is a research index, not an endorsement or a promise of earnings. Rates may apply only to particular plans, first purchases, a fixed number of months, or approved partners. Some entries are partner or credit referral programs rather than standard cash affiliate programs; the individual row explains the distinction where it could be established.

For a correction or addition, provide the company name, main website, official affiliate application, current commission rate and duration, cookie window, a primary source, and the date checked. Include a note when terms require an approved account. Avoid submitting personal tracking links.
